It controls epistemic status and containment, never evidence visibility or presumptive acceptance/rejection.","legacy_docket_assessment":{"adjudicable":false,"packet":null,"status":"invalid"},"packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity":[{"focus_ref":"98:2","identity_status":"legacy_unbound","packet_identity":null,"protocol":"focus-trace-hermetic-response-v4","reader_id":"reader_hft_a","trace_kind":"reconstructed"}]},"reader_synthesis_count":9,"source_present":true,"structured_insight_count":11,"unstructured_record_count":0},"identity":{"ayah_ref":"98:2","lane":"global","linguistic_source_ref":"98:2","surface_ref":"98:2","unit_kind":"numbered_ayah"},"primary_floor":{"source_ref":"98:2","target_tokens":[["Allah'tan",["98:2:2","98:2:3"]],["bir",["98:2:1"]],["elçi",["98:2:1"]],["arındırılmış",["98:2:6"]],["sayfalar",["98:2:5"]],["okur",["98:2:4"]]],"text":"Allah'tan bir elçi, arındırılmış sayfalar okur."},"schema_version":"commentary-v5-hermetic-scope-packet-v1","scope":{"hft":{"assigned_record_count":9,"authoring_policy":{"every_assigned_record_requires_explicit_review":true,"parseable_hft_is_always_visible_to_authoring":true,"provenance_affects_qualification_not_visibility":true,"scope_mismatch_affects_lane_assignment_not_visibility":true,"unresolved_branch_citations_do_not_suppress_records":true},"authoring_status":"visible_with_provenance_qualification"},"lane_contract":"reader walks and cross-run publications with explicit trust labels","pericope":{"ayah_from":1,"ayah_to":8,"id":"s098-p01-001-008","label":"Whole surah","number":1,"refs":["98:1","98:2","98:3","98:4","98:5","98:6","98:7","98:8"]},"readiness":{"authoring_effect":"Docket readiness is reported for source provenance. HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000186/B003","root_000186/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0388c26f9669e228a780","text":"2. **Recitation as following up a prior liability.** Evidence: ت ل و B003 remainder after what came before and B004 liability/right that follows its holder; ك ت ب in 98:1/3/4 as book/decree/register; د ي ن in 98:5 B003 debt and B002 reckoning. Structural cue: the messenger comes to أهل الكتاب and recites sheets “in which” are books. Assigned role: يتلو activates a trailing claim from prior written obligation. Abductive move: I supply the legal continuity: what is recited now follows and calls due what was already in the book-community. Reading change: recitation becomes pursuit of a remaining obligation, not just public reading.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000637/B002","root_000953/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3258e038b1b20eb30576","text":"4. **A purified carrier that is also purifying.** **Lexical evidence:** `مُّطَهَّرَة` anchors `ط ه ر` B001, “purity with defilement removed,” and activates B004, “a medium pure in itself and able to purify another,” plus B005, cleansing conduct from blame. **Structural cue and role:** 98:5 moves from the pure sheets to exclusive devotion, prayer, and `زَكَاة` (`ز ك و` B002, purity and rectitude), while 98:7 names sound acts. The sheets’ purity therefore receives a downstream ethical role. **Abductive move:** I infer transfer from purified medium to purified practice; the passive form states the carrier’s condition, while the purifying effect is an inferred activation. **Reading change:** `مُّطَهَّرَة` marks not only protected textual integrity but a carrier capable of reorganizing motive, worship, and social giving.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000186/B003","root_000186/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_342b7769674fd98343ab","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:2:4\",\"root_000186\",[\"B003\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Okuma, önceki yazılı yükümlülükten kalan ve sahibini izleyen bir hakkı yeniden gündeme getiren takip olarak da yorumlanabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000845/B002","root_000845/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3d4d1f567d92d7231612","text":"3. **Material plurality held in coherent collection.** **Lexical evidence:** `صُحُف` anchors `ص ح ف` B002, “written sheet,” while B001 supplies “flat spread and breadth” and B003 “collected sheets in a codex.” **Structural cue and role:** plural sheets contain plural `كُتُب` in 98:3, yet those writings are called `قَيِّمَة`; by contrast, human recipients scatter in 98:4. The sheet/codex branches make textual plurality a model of collection without fragmentation. **Abductive move:** I infer that ordered containment, rather than mere plurality, differentiates coherent text from a scattered group. **Reading change:** the sheets become a materially distributed but functionally integrated carrier, intensifying the irony of recipients who fail to reproduce that integration.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000953/B001","root_000953/B004","root_000953/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_40bd009d823534103f1f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:2:6\",\"root_000953\",[\"B001\",\"B004\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sayfaların arınmışlığı yalnızca metnin korunmuş durumunu değil, niyeti, kulluğu ve vermeyi arındırabilecek bir taşıyıcı oluşunu da anlatır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000563/B001","root_000563/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_71e1d6c3200df8a6b2b7","text":"1. **The messenger as the mobile body of the proof.** **Lexical evidence:** `رَسُول` anchors `ر س ل` B001, “being sent forth and released,” and B002, “messenger and carried message.” **Structural cue and role:** 98:1 ends with the proof’s arrival; 98:2 immediately apposes a messenger from God who voices sheets, and 98:3 opens their contents. The dispatch branch turns the apparent object “proof” into a transmission chain: sender, envoy, voiced carrier, contained writings. **Abductive move:** I infer that the appositional sequence identifies the messenger-process as the arriving proof; the packet does not explicitly insert an equation sign. **Reading change:** the proof is read dynamically as authorized delivery, not as a self-contained proposition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000845/B001","root_000845/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b44ddc3cc99b8460097a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:2:5\",\"root_000845\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Çoğul sayfaların tek bir düzenli taşıyıcıda toplanması, alıcıların sonraki dağılmasına karşı parçalanmadan çoğul kalabilen bir bütünlük gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000845/B003","root_000953/B004","root_000953/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bc6dfcfe7cb6b60ffcb3","text":"98:4 made the material organization of 98:2 newly consequential: coherent plural sheets are followed by socially scattered recipients, so `ص ح ف` B003 became a contrastive image rather than a codex detail. 98:5 and 98:7 then extended `ط ه ر` B004 and B005 from carrier integrity into exclusive motive, purifying-giving practice, and sound action. The later sequence also corrected `يَتْلُوا` from voice alone to a proposed chain of following whose success or failure appears in conduct.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000186/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_cbbe0a64da1d40426410","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:2:4\",\"root_000186\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Okuma, sözleri sıraya koymakla kalmaz; bildirilen düzenin davranışta da izlenmesini isteyen bir takip zinciri kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_cc4319db4c952c624fcc","text":"98:5 made مطهّرة less static: إخلاص، صلاة، زكاة show purification enacted in worship and giving. 98:3 also corrected the reading of صحف: the sheets are containers of “books,” so the visible page is a vessel for binding upright content.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000563/B001","root_000563/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e410f070877ca1d3f9bc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:2:1\",\"root_000563\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Elçi, açık kanıtı durağan bir önerme olmaktan çıkarıp gönderen, taşıyan ses ve yazılı içerikten oluşan hareketli bir iletim sürecine dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000845/B001","root_000845/B004","root_000953/B004","root_000953/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e567f51fd46a262a2b3b","text":"3. **Purified sheets as cleansing medium.** Evidence: ط ه ر B004 purifying water/medium and B005 cleansing conduct; ص ح ف B001 flat spread/breadth and B004 shallow basin; ز ك و in 98:5 B002 purity/rectitude. Structural cue: purification language returns in sincere religion and zakah. Assigned role: the sheets are a surface that can cleanse conduct by exposing and ordering it. Abductive move: I infer an analogy between clean textual surface and ritual/ethical purification. Reading change: مطهّرة describes not only the documents’ status but their function in cleansing the readers’ mixed condition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000186/B001","root_000563/B001","root_000845/B002","root_000953/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ed349c5d901246b6facf","text":"1. **The messenger as released carrier of ordered speech.** Evidence: ر س ل B001 being sent/released, B002 messenger/message; ت ل و B001 following in sequence; ص ح ف B002 written sheets; ط ه ر B001 purity. Structural cue: 98:1 names the proof; 98:2 identifies it as a messenger reciting; 98:3 locates upright books inside the sheets. Assigned role: the messenger is the release-channel, recitation is ordered following, sheets are the written surface, purity protects transmission. Abductive move: I infer that “proof” becomes effective through an embodied carrier plus sequential recitation. Reading change: the bayyina is not a bare miracle-sign; it is a sent, recited, purified textual process.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000186/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f02ac62f6681fe803b15","text":"2. **Recitation as ordered following that seeks further following.** **Lexical evidence:** `يَتْلُوا` anchors `ت ل و` B001, “following in sequence.” **Structural cue and role:** the verb sits between the sent messenger and plural sheets; after it, contents become upright writings (98:3), command becomes ordered practice (98:5), and faith is paired with action (98:7). The branch supplies the sequencing operation that carries order from source to voice and potentially from voice to conduct. **Abductive move:** I infer that voiced sequence is meant to generate enacted sequence; the later acts show correspondence, not an explicit causal statement. **Reading change:** `يَتْلُوا` exceeds vocal performance and becomes the making-present of an order whose adequacy is tested by whether recipients follow it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
